                "explanation": "感觉性需求是指社区居民或服务对象感受到或意识到，并用言语表述出来的需要。表达性需 求是指社区居民或服务对象把自身的感党通过行动表达出来的需要。规范性需求是指由专家学者、专 业人士、政府行政官员评估而决定的需求。比较性需求是指社区居民或服务对象将所得到的服务与 其他类似社区进行比较，而认为有所差别的需要。题目中“居民们说，这里胡同狭罕，私家车越来越 多，通行越来越不安全”属于感觉性需求; “早晨胡同早点摊前排起的长龙常常堵塞交通”属于表达 性需求; “街道办事处的调查报告也认为该社区环境和设施需要规划重整”属于规范性需求。"

/**
 * 社工考试密押题库系统 - 主程序逻辑
 * 支持多书籍、独立进度、遗忘曲线算法
 */

// ==================== 全局配置 ====================
const CONFIG = {
    // 遗忘曲线配置（天数）
    FORGETTING_CURVE: {
        HIGH: 3,    // 高优先级：3天内需要复习
        MEDIUM: 7,  // 中优先级：7天内需要复习
        LOW: 14     // 低优先级：14天内掌握
    },
    // 每次练习题目数量
    QUIZ_SIZE: 20,
    // 书籍数据已嵌入EMBEDDED_BOOKS变量中
    BOOKS_CONFIG: null,
    BOOKS_PATH: null
};

// ==================== 全局状态 ====================
let appState = {
    // 当前选中的书籍
    currentBook: null,
    // 当前书籍数据
    bookData: null,
    // 用户数据（按书籍分开存储）
    userData: null,
    // 当前练习模式
    practiceMode: null,
    // 当前试卷ID
    currentPaperId: null,
    // 当前练习题目列表
    quizQuestions: [],
    // 当前题目索引
    currentIndex: 0,
    // 用户答案记录
    userAnswers: {},
    // 练习结果
    quizResult: null
};

// ==================== 书籍管理 ====================

/**
 * 加载书籍列表配置（使用嵌入的数据）
 */
async function loadBooksConfig() {
    // 直接从嵌入的数据中获取书籍列表
    return {
        books: EMBEDDED_BOOKS.books.map(book => ({
            id: book.id,
            title: book.title,
            description: book.description,
            file: book.id + '.json',
            totalCount: book.totalCount
        }))
    };
}


/**
 * 显示书籍选择列表
 */
async function showBookList() {
    const bookListEl = document.getElementById('bookList');
    bookListEl.innerHTML = '<div class="loading">正在加载书籍列表...</div>';
    
    try {
        const config = await loadBooksConfig();
        const books = config.books;
        
        if (books.length === 0) {
            bookListEl.innerHTML = '<div class="error-message">暂无可用的书籍</div>';
            return;
        }
        
        let html = '';
        for (const book of books) {
            const progress = getBookProgressSummary(book.id);
            const progressPercent = book.totalCount > 0 
                ? Math.round((progress.learned / book.totalCount) * 100) 
                : 0;
            
            html += `
                <div class="book-card" onclick="selectBook('${book.id}')">
                    <div class="book-icon">📚</div>
                    <div class="book-info">
                        <div class="book-title">${book.title}</div>
                        <div class="book-desc">${book.description || ''}</div>
                        <div class="book-progress">
                            <div class="mini-progress-bar">
                                <div class="mini-progress-fill" style="width: ${progressPercent}%"></div>
                            </div>
                            <span class="mini-progress-text">${progress.learned}/${book.totalCount}题 (${progressPercent}%)</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="book-arrow">›</div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        bookListEl.innerHTML = html;
    } catch (error) {
        bookListEl.innerHTML = `<div class="error-message">加载失败: ${error.message}</div>`;
    }
}

/**
 * 获取书籍进度摘要
 */
function getBookProgressSummary(bookId) {
    const key = `swg_exam_${bookId}`;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!saved) {
        return { learned: 0, mastered: 0 };
    }
    
    try {
        const data = JSON.parse(saved);
        const progress = data.progress || {};
        const learned = Object.keys(progress).length;
        let mastered = 0;
        
        for (const qId in progress) {
            if (progress[qId].level === 'mastered') {
                mastered++;
            }
        }
        
        return { learned, mastered };
    } catch (e) {
        return { learned: 0, mastered: 0 };
    }
}

/**
 * 选择书籍（使用嵌入的数据）
 */
async function selectBook(bookId) {
    showLoading('正在加载书籍...');
    
    try {
        // 从嵌入的数据中查找书籍
        const bookInfo = EMBEDDED_BOOKS.books.find(b => b.id === bookId);
        if (!bookInfo) {
            throw new Error('找不到书籍数据');
        }
        
        appState.bookData = bookInfo.data;
        appState.currentBook = bookId;
        appState.userData = loadUserData(bookId);
        
        hideLoading();
        showScreen('homeScreen');
        updateHomeScreen();
    } catch (error) {
        hideLoading();
        alert('加载书籍失败: ' + error.message);
    }
}

// ==================== 用户数据管理 ====================

/**
 * 加载用户数据（按书籍分开）
 */
function loadUserData(bookId) {
    const key = `swg_exam_${bookId}`;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(key);
    
    if (saved) {
        try {
            return JSON.parse(saved);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('解析用户数据失败:', e);
        }
    }
    
    // 返回默认数据结构
    return {
        progress: {},      // 题目进度 { questionId: { level, lastStudy, nextReview, correct, wrong } }
        history: [],       // 学习历史记录
        stats: {           // 统计数据
            total: 0,
            correct: 0,
            wrong: 0
        }
    };
}

/**
 * 保存用户数据
 */
function saveUserData() {
    if (!appState.currentBook) return;
    
    const key = `swg_exam_${appState.currentBook}`;
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(appState.userData));
}

/**
 * 保存练习进度
 */
function saveQuizProgress() {
    if (!appState.currentBook || !appState.practiceMode) return;
    
    const progressData = {
        bookId: appState.currentBook,
        practiceMode: appState.practiceMode,
        currentPaperId: appState.currentPaperId,
        questions: appState.quizQuestions,
        currentIndex: appState.currentIndex,
        userAnswers: appState.userAnswers,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    };
    
    localStorage.setItem('swg_exam_quiz_progress', JSON.stringify(progressData));
}

/**
 * 加载练习进度
 */
function loadQuizProgress() {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('swg_exam_quiz_progress');
    if (!saved) return null;
    
    try {
        return JSON.parse(saved);
    } catch (e) {
        return null;
    }
}

/**
 * 清除练习进度
 */
function clearQuizProgress() {
    localStorage.removeItem('swg_exam_quiz_progress');
}

/**
 * 检查是否有未完成的练习
 */
function hasUnfinishedQuiz() {
    const progress = loadQuizProgress();
    if (!progress) return false;
    
    // 检查是否真的未完成
    const total = progress.questions ? progress.questions.length : 0;
    const current = progress.currentIndex || 0;
    return current < total;
}

/**
 * 继续上次练习
 */
function continueQuiz() {
    const progress = loadQuizProgress();
    if (!progress) return;
    
    // 加载书籍
    const book = EMBEDDED_BOOKS.books.find(b => b.id === progress.bookId);
    if (!book) {
        alert('无法找到原书籍');
        clearQuizProgress();
        return;
    }
    
    appState.currentBook = progress.bookId;
    appState.bookData = book.data;
    appState.userData = loadUserData(progress.bookId);
    appState.practiceMode = progress.practiceMode;
    appState.currentPaperId = progress.currentPaperId;
    appState.quizQuestions = progress.questions;
    appState.currentIndex = progress.currentIndex;
    appState.userAnswers = progress.userAnswers || {};
    
    showScreen('quizScreen');
    renderQuestion();
}

/**
 * 更新题目进度
 */
function updateQuestionProgress(questionId, isCorrect) {
    const progress = appState.userData.progress;
    const now = Date.now();
    
    if (!progress[questionId]) {
        progress[questionId] = {
            level: 'new',
            lastStudy: null,
            nextReview: null,
            correct: 0,
            wrong: 0,
            consecutiveCorrect: 0  // 新增：连续正确次数
        };
    }
    
    const qProgress = progress[questionId];
    qProgress.lastStudy = now;
    
    if (isCorrect) {
        qProgress.correct++;
        qProgress.consecutiveCorrect++;  // 增加连续正确次数
        
        // 连续两次全对，标记为已掌握
        if (qProgress.consecutiveCorrect >= 2) {
            qProgress.level = 'mastered';
            qProgress.nextReview = now + CONFIG.FORGETTING_CURVE.LOW * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        } else if (qProgress.correct >= 1) {
            qProgress.level = 'learning';
            qProgress.nextReview = now + CONFIG.FORGETTING_CURVE.MEDIUM * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        }
    } else {
        qProgress.wrong++;
        qProgress.consecutiveCorrect = 0;  // 重置连续正确次数
        qProgress.level = 'weak';
        qProgress.nextReview = now + CONFIG.FORGETTING_CURVE.HIGH * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    }
    
    // 更新统计
    appState.userData.stats.total++;
    if (isCorrect) {
        appState.userData.stats.correct++;
    } else {
        appState.userData.stats.wrong++;
    }
    
    saveUserData();
    saveQuizProgress();  // 保存练习进度
}

// ==================== 首页更新 ====================

/**
 * 更新首页显示
 */
function updateHomeScreen() {
    if (!appState.bookData) return;
    
    // 更新书籍标题
    document.getElementById('currentBookTitle').textContent = appState.bookData.title;
    
    // 计算统计数据
    const progress = appState.userData.progress;
    const totalCount = appState.bookData.totalCount;
    
    let mastered = 0, learning = 0, weak = 0;
    
    for (const qId in progress) {
        const qProgress = progress[qId];
        if (qProgress.level === 'mastered') {
            mastered++;
        } else if (qProgress.level === 'learning') {
            learning++;
        } else if (qProgress.level === 'weak') {
            weak++;
        }
    }
    
    const learned = mastered + learning + weak;
    const masterRate = learned > 0 ? Math.round((mastered / learned) * 100) : 0;
    
    // 更新统计卡片
    document.getElementById('masteredCount').textContent = mastered;
    document.getElementById('learningCount').textContent = learning;
    document.getElementById('mistakeCount').textContent = weak;
    document.getElementById('masterRate').textContent = masterRate + '%';
    
    // 更新进度条
    const progressPercent = totalCount > 0 ? Math.round((learned / totalCount) * 100) : 0;
    document.getElementById('totalProgress').style.width = progressPercent + '%';
    document.getElementById('progressText').textContent = `${learned} / ${totalCount} 题`;
    
    // 更新遗忘曲线状态
    const now = Date.now();
    let needReview = 0, studying = 0, mastered2 = 0;
    
    for (const qId in progress) {
        const qProgress = progress[qId];
        if (qProgress.nextReview && qProgress.nextReview <= now) {
            needReview++;
        } else if (qProgress.level === 'mastered') {
            mastered2++;
        } else {
            studying++;
        }
    }
    
    const curveTotal = Math.max(needReview + studying + mastered2, 1);
    document.getElementById('curveHigh').style.width = Math.round((needReview / curveTotal) * 100) + '%';
    document.getElementById('curveHighText').textContent = Math.round((needReview / curveTotal) * 100) + '%';
    document.getElementById('curveMedium').style.width = Math.round((studying / curveTotal) * 100) + '%';
    document.getElementById('curveMediumText').textContent = Math.round((studying / curveTotal) * 100) + '%';
    document.getElementById('curveLow').style.width = Math.round((mastered2 / curveTotal) * 100) + '%';
    document.getElementById('curveLowText').textContent = Math.round((mastered2 / curveTotal) * 100) + '%';
    
    // 更新试卷列表
    updatePaperList();
    
    // 检查是否有未完成的练习
    const unfinishedProgress = loadQuizProgress();
    const continueCard = document.getElementById('continueCard');
    if (continueCard && unfinishedProgress && unfinishedProgress.bookId === appState.currentBook) {
        const total = unfinishedProgress.questions ? unfinishedProgress.questions.length : 0;
        const current = unfinishedProgress.currentIndex || 0;
        if (current < total) {
            continueCard.style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('continueIndex').textContent = current + 1;
            document.getElementById('continueRemaining').textContent = total - current;
        } else {
            continueCard.style.display = 'none';
        }
    } else if (continueCard) {
        continueCard.style.display = 'none';
    }
}

/**
 * 更新试卷列表
 */
function updatePaperList() {
    const paperListEl = document.getElementById('paperList');
    const papers = appState.bookData.papers || [];
    
    if (papers.length === 0) {
        paperListEl.innerHTML = '<div class="no-data">暂无试卷</div>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    papers.forEach((paper, index) => {
        const singleCount = paper.singleChoice ? paper.singleChoice.length : 0;
        const multiCount = paper.multipleChoice ? paper.multipleChoice.length : 0;
        const totalCount = singleCount + multiCount;
        
        html += `
            <div class="paper-item" onclick="selectPaper('${paper.id}')">
                <div class="paper-icon">📋</div>
                <div class="paper-info">
                    <div class="paper-title">${paper.title}</div>
                    <div class="paper-meta">单选 ${singleCount} 题 / 多选 ${multiCount} 题</div>
                </div>
                <div class="paper-arrow">›</div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    paperListEl.innerHTML = html;
}

// ==================== 练习模式 ====================

/**
 * 开始练习
 */
function startPractice(mode) {
    appState.practiceMode = mode;
    appState.currentPaperId = null;
    
    // 获取所有题目
    const allQuestions = getAllQuestions();
    
    // 根据模式筛选题目
    let selectedQuestions = [];
    
    switch (mode) {
        case 'smart':
            selectedQuestions = smartSelectQuestions(allQuestions);
            break;
        case 'weak':
            selectedQuestions = weakSelectQuestions(allQuestions);
            break;
        case 'random':
            selectedQuestions = randomSelectQuestions(allQuestions);
            break;
        case 'new':
            selectedQuestions = newSelectQuestions(allQuestions);
            break;
        default:
            selectedQuestions = randomSelectQuestions(allQuestions);
    }
    
    if (selectedQuestions.length === 0) {
        alert('没有符合条件的题目，请尝试其他练习模式');
        return;
    }
    
    // 开始答题
    startQuiz(selectedQuestions);
}

/**
 * 选择试卷
 */
function selectPaper(paperId) {
    appState.practiceMode = 'paper';
    appState.currentPaperId = paperId;
    
    // 获取试卷题目
    const paper = appState.bookData.papers.find(p => p.id === paperId);
    if (!paper) {
        alert('试卷不存在');
        return;
    }
    
    // 合并单选和多选题目
    let questions = [];
    if (paper.singleChoice) {
        questions = questions.concat(paper.singleChoice.map(q => ({ ...q, questionType: 'single' })));
    }
    if (paper.multipleChoice) {
        questions = questions.concat(paper.multipleChoice.map(q => ({ ...q, questionType: 'multiple' })));
    }
    
    if (questions.length === 0) {
        alert('试卷没有题目');
        return;
    }
    
    startQuiz(questions);
}

/**
 * 获取所有题目
 */
function getAllQuestions() {
    const papers = appState.bookData.papers || [];
    let allQuestions = [];
    
    papers.forEach(paper => {
        if (paper.singleChoice) {
            allQuestions = allQuestions.concat(paper.singleChoice.map(q => ({ ...q, questionType: 'single' })));
        }
        if (paper.multipleChoice) {
            allQuestions = allQuestions.concat(paper.multipleChoice.map(q => ({ ...q, questionType: 'multiple' })));
        }
    });
    
    return allQuestions;
}

/**
 * 智能选题（基于遗忘曲线）
 */
function smartSelectQuestions(allQuestions) {
    const now = Date.now();
    const progress = appState.userData.progress;
    
    // 分类题目
    const needReview = [];
    const learning = [];
    const newQuestions = [];
    
    allQuestions.forEach(q => {
        const qProgress = progress[q.id];
        if (!qProgress) {
            newQuestions.push(q);
        } else if (qProgress.nextReview && qProgress.nextReview <= now) {
            needReview.push({ ...q, priority: qProgress.level === 'weak' ? 3 : 2 });
        } else if (qProgress.level !== 'mastered') {
            learning.push({ ...q, priority: 1 });
        }
    });
    
    // 按优先级排序
    needReview.sort((a, b) => b.priority - a.priority);
    
    // 组合题目：优先复习，然后学习中，最后新题
    let selected = [];
    selected = selected.concat(needReview.slice(0, 10));
    selected = selected.concat(learning.slice(0, 5));
    selected = selected.concat(newQuestions.slice(0, 5));
    
    // 如果不够，随机补充
    if (selected.length < CONFIG.QUIZ_SIZE) {
        const remaining = allQuestions.filter(q => !selected.find(s => s.id === q.id));
        selected = selected.concat(shuffleArray(remaining).slice(0, CONFIG.QUIZ_SIZE - selected.length));
    }
    
    return shuffleArray(selected.slice(0, CONFIG.QUIZ_SIZE));
}

/**
 * 薄弱强化选题
 */
function weakSelectQuestions(allQuestions) {
    const progress = appState.userData.progress;
    
    // 筛选错题和薄弱题目
    const weakQuestions = allQuestions.filter(q => {
        const qProgress = progress[q.id];
        return qProgress && (qProgress.level === 'weak' || qProgress.wrong > 0);
    });
    
    if (weakQuestions.length === 0) {
        alert('太棒了！没有需要强化的题目');
        return [];
    }
    
    return shuffleArray(weakQuestions).slice(0, CONFIG.QUIZ_SIZE);
}

/**
 * 随机选题
 */
function randomSelectQuestions(allQuestions) {
    const progress = appState.userData.progress;
    
    // 过滤掉已掌握的题目（连续两次全对）
    const availableQuestions = allQuestions.filter(q => {
        const qProgress = progress[q.id];
        // 如果没有进度记录，或者是未掌握状态，则包含
        return !qProgress || qProgress.level !== 'mastered';
    });
    
    if (availableQuestions.length === 0) {
        alert('太棒了！所有题目都已掌握，可以尝试其他练习模式');
        return [];
    }
    
    return shuffleArray(availableQuestions).slice(0, CONFIG.QUIZ_SIZE);
}

/**
 * 新题选题
 */
function newSelectQuestions(allQuestions) {
    const progress = appState.userData.progress;
    
    // 筛选未学习题目
    const newQuestions = allQuestions.filter(q => !progress[q.id]);
    
    if (newQuestions.length === 0) {
        alert('恭喜！所有题目都已学习过');
        return [];
    }
    
    return shuffleArray(newQuestions).slice(0, CONFIG.QUIZ_SIZE);
}

/**
 * 数组随机排序
 */
function shuffleArray(array) {
    const arr = [...array];
    for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
        [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
    }
    return arr;
}

// ==================== 答题界面 ====================

/**
 * 开始答题
 */
function startQuiz(questions) {
    appState.quizQuestions = questions;
    appState.currentIndex = 0;
    appState.userAnswers = {};
    appState.quizResult = null;
    
    showScreen('quizScreen');
    renderQuestion();
}

/**
 * 渲染当前题目
 */
function renderQuestion() {
    const question = appState.quizQuestions[appState.currentIndex];
    const total = appState.quizQuestions.length;
    const current = appState.currentIndex + 1;
    
    // 更新进度
    document.getElementById('questionNum').textContent = `第 ${current} 题 / 共 ${total} 题`;
    document.getElementById('quizProgressFill').style.width = `${(current / total) * 100}%`;
    
    // 更新题目类型标签
    const typeLabel = question.questionType === 'multiple' ? '多选题' : '单选题';
    document.getElementById('questionType').textContent = typeLabel;
    document.getElementById('questionKnowledge').textContent = question.knowledge || '知识点';
    
    // 更新题目内容
    document.getElementById('questionText').textContent = question.question;
    
    // 渲染选项
    renderOptions(question);
    
    // 隐藏解析
    document.getElementById('explanationCard').style.display = 'none';
    
    // 更新按钮状态
    updateQuizButtons();
    
    // 恢复已选答案
    restoreUserAnswer(question);
}

/**
 * 渲染选项
 */
function renderOptions(question) {
    const optionsListEl = document.getElementById('optionsList');
    const options = question.options || [];
    const letters = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E'];
    
    let html = '';
    options.forEach((option, index) => {
        const letter = letters[index];
        html += `
            <div class="option-item" data-letter="${letter}" onclick="selectOption('${letter}', '${question.questionType}')">
                <span class="option-letter">${letter}</span>
                <span class="option-text">${option}</span>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    optionsListEl.innerHTML = html;
}

/**
 * 选择选项
 */
function selectOption(letter, questionType) {
    const question = appState.quizQuestions[appState.currentIndex];
    const questionId = question.id;
    
    // 如果已经查看过答案，不允许再修改
    if (appState.userAnswers[`checked_${questionId}`]) {
        return;
    }
    
    if (questionType === 'multiple') {
        // 多选题：切换选中状态
        if (!appState.userAnswers[questionId]) {
            appState.userAnswers[questionId] = [];
        }
        const index = appState.userAnswers[questionId].indexOf(letter);
        if (index > -1) {
            appState.userAnswers[questionId].splice(index, 1);
        } else {
            appState.userAnswers[questionId].push(letter);
            appState.userAnswers[questionId].sort();
        }
    } else {
        // 单选题：直接设置答案并立即检查
        appState.userAnswers[questionId] = letter;
        updateOptionUI();
        // 立即显示答案
        setTimeout(() => checkAnswer(), 300);
        return;
    }
    
    updateOptionUI();
}

/**
 * 更新选项UI
 */
function updateOptionUI() {
    const question = appState.quizQuestions[appState.currentIndex];
    const questionId = question.id;
    const userAnswer = appState.userAnswers[questionId];
    
    document.querySelectorAll('.option-item').forEach(el => {
        const letter = el.dataset.letter;
        el.classList.remove('selected');
        
        if (userAnswer) {
            if (Array.isArray(userAnswer)) {
                if (userAnswer.includes(letter)) {
                    el.classList.add('selected');
                }
            } else if (userAnswer === letter) {
                el.classList.add('selected');
            }
        }
    });
}

/**
 * 恢复用户已选答案
 */
function restoreUserAnswer(question) {
    const userAnswer = appState.userAnswers[question.id];
    if (!userAnswer) return;
    
    updateOptionUI();
}

/**
 * 更新答题按钮状态
 */
function updateQuizButtons() {
    const total = appState.quizQuestions.length;
    const current = appState.currentIndex;
    const question = appState.quizQuestions[current];
    const isLast = current === total - 1;
    
    // 上一题按钮
    document.getElementById('prevBtn').disabled = current === 0;
    
    // 如果已经查看过答案，显示下一题
    if (appState.userAnswers[`checked_${question.id}`]) {
        document.getElementById('checkBtn').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('confirmBtn').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('nextBtn').style.display = isLast ? 'none' : 'inline-block';
        document.getElementById('submitBtn').style.display = isLast ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
    } else {
        // 根据题目类型显示不同按钮
        if (question.questionType === 'multiple') {
            // 多选题：显示确认答案按钮
            document.getElementById('checkBtn').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('confirmBtn').style.display = 'inline-block';
        } else {
            // 单选题：隐藏按钮（会自动检查）
            document.getElementById('checkBtn').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('confirmBtn').style.display = 'none';
        }
        document.getElementById('nextBtn').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('submitBtn').style.display = 'none';
    }
}

/**
 * 查看答案
 */
function checkAnswer() {
    const question = appState.quizQuestions[appState.currentIndex];
    const questionId = question.id;
    const userAnswer = appState.userAnswers[questionId];
    
    if (!userAnswer || (Array.isArray(userAnswer) && userAnswer.length === 0)) {
        alert('请先选择答案');
        return;
    }
    
    // 标记已查看
    appState.userAnswers[`checked_${questionId}`] = true;
    
    // 判断是否正确
    const correctAnswer = question.answer;
    let isCorrect = false;
    
    if (question.questionType === 'multiple') {
        const userAnswerStr = Array.isArray(userAnswer) ? userAnswer.join('') : userAnswer;
        isCorrect = userAnswerStr === correctAnswer;
    } else {
        isCorrect = userAnswer === correctAnswer;
    }
    
    // 更新进度
    updateQuestionProgress(questionId, isCorrect);
    
    // 显示解析
    showExplanation(question, userAnswer, correctAnswer, isCorrect);
    
    // 更新按钮
    document.getElementById('checkBtn').style.display = 'none';
    updateQuizButtons();
}

/**
 * 显示解析
 */
function showExplanation(question, userAnswer, correctAnswer, isCorrect) {
    const explanationCard = document.getElementById('explanationCard');
    const answerResult = document.getElementById('answerResult');
    
    // 更新结果标签
    answerResult.textContent = isCorrect ? '回答正确' : '回答错误';
    answerResult.className = 'answer-result ' + (isCorrect ? 'correct' : 'wrong');
    
    // 更新解析内容
    document.getElementById('explanationText').textContent = question.explanation || '暂无解析';
    
    // 更新正确答案
    document.getElementById('correctAnswer').textContent = correctAnswer;
    
    // 显示解析卡片
    explanationCard.style.display = 'block';
    
    // 高亮选项
    highlightOptions(question, userAnswer, correctAnswer);
}

/**
 * 高亮选项
 */
function highlightOptions(question, userAnswer, correctAnswer) {
    const correctLetters = correctAnswer.split('');
    const userLetters = Array.isArray(userAnswer) ? userAnswer : [userAnswer];
    
    document.querySelectorAll('.option-item').forEach(el => {
        const letter = el.dataset.letter;
        el.classList.remove('correct', 'wrong', 'selected');
        
        if (correctLetters.includes(letter)) {
            el.classList.add('correct');
        } else if (userLetters.includes(letter)) {
            el.classList.add('wrong');
        }
    });
}

/**
 * 上一题
 */
function prevQuestion() {
    if (appState.currentIndex > 0) {
        appState.currentIndex--;
        renderQuestion();
    }
}

/**
 * 下一题
 */
function nextQuestion() {
    if (appState.currentIndex < appState.quizQuestions.length - 1) {
        appState.currentIndex++;
        renderQuestion();
    }
}

/**
 * 交卷
 */
function submitQuiz() {
    // 检查是否有未作答的题目
    const unanswered = appState.quizQuestions.filter(q => {
        const answer = appState.userAnswers[q.id];
        return !answer || (Array.isArray(answer) && answer.length === 0);
    });
    
    if (unanswered.length > 0) {
        if (!confirm(`还有 ${unanswered.length} 道题目未作答，确定要交卷吗？`)) {
            return;
        }
    }
    
    // 计算结果
    let correct = 0, wrong = 0;
    
    appState.quizQuestions.forEach(q => {
        const userAnswer = appState.userAnswers[q.id];
        const correctAnswer = q.answer;
        
        let isCorrect = false;
        if (userAnswer) {
            if (q.questionType === 'multiple') {
                const userAnswerStr = Array.isArray(userAnswer) ? userAnswer.join('') : userAnswer;
                isCorrect = userAnswerStr === correctAnswer;
            } else {
                isCorrect = userAnswer === correctAnswer;
            }
        }
        
        if (isCorrect) {
            correct++;
        } else {
            wrong++;
        }
    });
    
    appState.quizResult = {
        correct,
        wrong,
        total: appState.quizQuestions.length,
        rate: Math.round((correct / appState.quizQuestions.length) * 100)
    };
    
    // 清除保存的进度
    clearQuizProgress();
    
    // 显示结果页面
    showResult();
}

/**
 * 显示结果
 */
function showResult() {
    const result = appState.quizResult;
    
    document.getElementById('resultCorrect').textContent = result.correct;
    document.getElementById('resultWrong').textContent = result.wrong;
    document.getElementById('resultRate').textContent = result.rate + '%';
    
    showScreen('resultScreen');
}

/**
 * 退出答题
 */
function exitQuiz() {
    // 检查是否有已答题目
    const answeredCount = Object.keys(appState.userAnswers).filter(k => !k.startsWith('checked_')).length;
    
    if (answeredCount > 0) {
        if (confirm('练习进度将自动保存，下次可以继续。确定要退出吗？')) {
            saveQuizProgress();
            showScreen('homeScreen');
            updateHomeScreen();
        }
    } else {
        showScreen('homeScreen');
        updateHomeScreen();
    }
}

/**
 * 查看错题
 */
function reviewMistakes() {
    // 筛选本次练习的错题
    const mistakes = appState.quizQuestions.filter(q => {
        const userAnswer = appState.userAnswers[q.id];
        const correctAnswer = q.answer;
        
        if (!userAnswer) return true;
        
        if (q.questionType === 'multiple') {
            const userAnswerStr = Array.isArray(userAnswer) ? userAnswer.join('') : userAnswer;
            return userAnswerStr !== correctAnswer;
        }
        
        return userAnswer !== correctAnswer;
    });
    
    if (mistakes.length === 0) {
        alert('太棒了！没有错题');
        backToHome();
        return;
    }
    
    // 重新开始错题练习
    startQuiz(mistakes);
}

/**
 * 返回首页
 */
function backToHome() {
    showScreen('homeScreen');
    updateHomeScreen();
}

/**
 * 返回书籍选择
 */
function showBookSelect() {
    appState.currentBook = null;
    appState.bookData = null;
    appState.userData = null;
    showScreen('bookSelectScreen');
    showBookList();
}

// ==================== 界面控制 ====================

/**
 * 显示指定屏幕
 */
function showScreen(screenId) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(screen => {
        screen.classList.remove('active');
    });
    document.getElementById(screenId).classList.add('active');
}

/**
 * 显示加载提示
 */
function showLoading(message) {
    // 简单实现，可以后续优化
    console.log('Loading:', message);
}

/**
 * 隐藏加载提示
 */
function hideLoading() {
    console.log('Loading complete');
}

// ==================== 初始化 ====================

/**
 * 应用初始化
 */
async function initApp() {
    console.log('社工考试密押题库系统初始化...');
    
    // 显示书籍选择列表
    await showBookList();
}

// 页面加载完成后初始化
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initApp);

// 注册 Service Worker
if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
    navigator.serviceWorker.register('./sw.js').then(function(reg) {
        console.log('Service Worker registered, scope:', reg.scope);
    }).catch(function(err) {
        console.log('Service Worker registration failed:', err);
    });
}
</script>
</body></html>
